❯ PRD 03 — Dynamic PersonDetail Routing & Safe URL Parameter Extraction         
  Development Task: Implement dynamic person-detail rendering using React       
  Router's useParams().                                                         
                                                                                
  Git Commit: Part of feat: detail route handling                               
                                                                                
  1. Objective                                                                  
  Implement PersonDetail.jsx as the dynamic detail component for the            
  Master-Detail Directory.                                                      
                                                                                
  The component must extract the selected person's ID from the URL using React  
  Router's useParams() hook.                                                    
                                                                                
  It must locate the corresponding person record using the existing people      
  dataset and render the person's information through semantic HTML definition  
  lists.                                                                        
                                                                                
  2. Functional Requirements                                                    
  2.1 Create PersonDetail.jsx                                                   
  Create or update PersonDetail.jsx.                                            
                                                                                
  Import useParams from React Router v7.                                        
                                                                                
  Extract the dynamic id route parameter.                                       
                                                                                
  Use the extracted ID to locate the corresponding person.                      
                                                                                
  Expected implementation pattern:                                              
                                                                                
  import { useParams } from 'react-router';                                     
                                                                                
  function PersonDetail({ people }) {                                           
    const { id } = useParams();                                                 
                                                                                
    const person = people.find(                                                 
      (p) => p.id === id                                                        
    );                                                                          
                                                                                
    // Detail rendering follows here                                            
  }                                                                             
  The people collection must come from the existing application data source.    
                                                                                
  Do not introduce a separate duplicate dataset.                                
                                                                                
  2.2 Safe URL Parameter Extraction                                             
  Use useParams() rather than manually reading window.location.                 
                                                                                
  Avoid manually splitting URL strings.                                         
                                                                                
  Ensure route IDs are interpreted consistently.                                
                                                                                
  Handle missing or unmatched IDs safely.                                       
                                                                                
  Do not assume that every URL contains a valid person ID.                      
                                                                                
  2.3 Person Record Lookup                                                      
  Use the required people.find() method:                                        
                                                                                
  const person = people.find(                                                   
    (p) => p.id === id                                                          
  );                                                                            
  Requirements:                                                                 
                                                                                
  Compare the dynamic route ID with the corresponding person ID.                
                                                                                
  Return the matching person record.                                            
                                                                                
  Do not mutate the original dataset.                                           
                                                                                
  Handle unmatched records through the Not Found state.                         
                                                                                
  2.4 Semantic HTML Definition Lists                                            
  Render person details using semantic HTML:                                    
                                                                                
  <dl> for the complete collection of descriptive information.                  
                                                                                
  <dt> for each field name.                                                     
                                                                                
  <dd> for its corresponding value.                                             
                                                                                
  Expected implementation:                                                      
                                                                                
  return (                                                                      
    <dl className="person-details">                                             
      <dt>Name:</dt>                                                            
      <dd>{person.name}</dd>                                                    
                                                                                
      <dt>Role:</dt>                                                            
      <dd>{person.role}</dd>                                                    
    </dl>                                                                       
  );                                                                            
  Requirements:                                                                 
                                                                                
  Use meaningful field labels.                                                  
                                                                                
  Preserve the relationship between each label and value.                       
                                                                                
  Avoid using generic paragraph elements as substitutes for definition-list     
  semantics.                                                                    
                                                                                
  3. Acceptance Criteria                                                        
                                                                                
  PersonDetail.jsx exists.                                                      
                                                                                
                                                                                
  useParams() is imported and implemented.                                      
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Dynamic ID extraction works through React Router.                             
                                                                                
                                                                                
  people.find() is used to locate the person.                                   
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Existing person data is reused.                                               
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Person information renders correctly.                                         
                                                                                
                                                                                
  <dl>, <dt>, and <dd> are implemented.                                         
                                                                                
                                                                                
  No unsafe manual URL parsing is introduced.                                   
                                                                                
                                                                                
  No console warnings appear.                                                   
                                                                                
  4. Out of Scope                                                               
  Creating a new backend API.                                                   
                                                                                
  Introducing a new database.                                                   
                                                                                
  Replacing the existing person dataset.                                        
                                                                                
  Implementing unrelated profile features.                                      
                                                                                
  PRD 04 — Not Found State, Invalid ID Handling & Route Validation              
  Development Task: Implement robust fallback behavior for invalid dynamic      
  routes and complete Sprint 1 integration testing.                             
                                                                                
  Git Commit: feat: detail route handling                                       
                                                                                
  1. Objective                                                                  
  Complete the dynamic detail-routing workflow by handling invalid or           
  nonexistent person IDs.                                                       
                                                                                
  The application must not attempt to render undefined person properties.       
                                                                                
  Instead, it must display a clear Not Found interface whenever the requested   
  person cannot be located.                                                     
                                                                                
  The final implementation must also pass the Sprint 1 DevTools checkpoints.    
                                                                                
  2. Functional Requirements                                                    
  2.1 NotFoundState Component                                                   
  Create or reuse a NotFoundState component.                                    
                                                                                
  Display a clear message when the requested person does not exist.             
                                                                                
  Provide a navigation link back to the directory.                              
                                                                                
  Use semantic HTML.                                                            
                                                                                
  Avoid displaying raw JavaScript errors or undefined values.                   
                                                                                
  Expected implementation:                                                      
                                                                                
  function NotFoundState() {                                                    
    return (                                                                    
      <section className="not-found">                                           
        <h2>Person Not Found</h2>                                               
                                                                                
        <p>                                                                     
          The requested person could not be found.                              
        </p>                                                                    
                                                                                
        <Link to="/people">                                                     
          Return to Directory                                                   
        </Link>                                                                 
      </section>                                                                
    );                                                                          
  }                                                                             
  2.2 Invalid ID Handling                                                       
  Integrate the fallback into PersonDetail:                                     
                                                                                
  if (!person) {                                                                
    return <NotFoundState />;                                                   
  }                                                                             
  Requirements:                                                                 
                                                                                
  Handle nonexistent IDs.                                                       
                                                                                
  Handle malformed or unexpected route parameters safely.                       
                                                                                
  Prevent attempts to access properties of undefined.                           
                                                                                
  Preserve the shared PeopleLayout structure.                                   
                                                                                
  Ensure the directory navigation remains available.                            
                                                                                
  2.3 Route Validation                                                          
  Test the following URL scenarios:                                             
                                                                                
  URL    Expected Result                                                        
  /people    Directory index                                                    
  /people/valid-id    Matching person detail                                    
  /people/invalid-id    Person Not Found                                        
  /people/nonexistent    Person Not Found                                       
  Use actual valid IDs from the existing dataset when testing.                  
                                                                                
  2.4 DevTools Validation                                                       
  The completed implementation must pass the Sprint 1 checkpoints.              
                                                                                
  Console validation:                                                           
                                                                                
  No React console warnings.                                                    
                                                                                
  No React Router warnings.                                                     
                                                                                
  No uncaught JavaScript errors.                                                
                                                                                
  No missing-key warnings from existing list rendering.                         
                                                                                
  URL validation:                                                               
                                                                                
  Dynamic route parameters are extracted through useParams().                   
                                                                                
  URL parsing does not depend on manual string manipulation.                    
                                                                                
  Valid IDs resolve to the correct person.                                      
                                                                                
  Invalid IDs render the Not Found state.                                       
                                                                                
  Responsive validation:                                                        
                                                                                
  The two-column CSS Grid layout works on desktop.                              
                                                                                
  The layout adapts to smaller screen widths.                                   
                                                                                
  No unexpected horizontal overflow occurs.                                     
                                                                                
  3. Acceptance Criteria                                                        
                                                                                
  NotFoundState is implemented or reused.

● Sprint 1 is complete. PRDs 03 and 04 are committed together as 529c3e3 feat: 
  detail route handling, along with the new folder structure. All 37 unit tests
  and 19 browser tests (Edge) pass, and the production build succeeds.

  Structure: files now sit in components/NotFoundState.jsx,
  layouts/PeopleLayout.jsx, pages/PeopleDirectory.jsx and
  pages/PersonDetail.jsx, plus data/people.js, App.jsx and main.jsx. Your tree
  had no CSS, so PeopleLayout.css sits next to its layout and index.css stays in
  src.

  PRD 03, the detail page (pages/PersonDetail.jsx):
  - It gets the id with const { id } = useParams() and looks it up with an exact
    people.find((p) => p.id === id). There's no window.location and no string
    splitting.
  - App.jsx passes the existing people data in (<PersonDetail people={people} 
    />), so there's no second copy of the data.
  - Every field (Name, Role, Email, Location, Bio, Skills) is a <dt>/<dd> pair
    inside <dl class="person-details">. On desktop each label sits beside its
    value; on phones the label goes above.

  PRD 04, the Not Found page (components/NotFoundState.jsx):
  - It shows a "Person Not Found" heading, a message and a "Return to Directory"
    link, with the sidebar still visible.
  - PersonDetail checks if (!person) return <NotFoundState /> before reading any
    field, so undefined is never displayed.
  - It deliberately doesn't repeat the id from the URL, so URL text never
    appears on the page.
  - My additions: paths like /people/p-01/extra also show Not Found inside the
    layout, and any other unknown path redirects to /people. Without these,
    React Router prints a "No routes matched" warning.

  Checks:
  - URL table: /people shows the directory, /people/p-01 to p-03 show the right
    person, and /people/invalid-id and /people/nonexistent show Not Found.
  - Tricky ids: __proto__, toString, P-01, ids with spaces, an HTML/script
    string and 1 all safely show Not Found. An encoded valid id (p%2D01)
    correctly opens the person.
  - In the browser: every URL in the table at 1280px and 375px, with the layout
    kept, no undefined on screen, no sideways scroll and a clean console. The
    "Return to Directory" link and back button also work.

  One exception to the no-warnings rule: a badly encoded URL such as
  /people/%E0%A4%A still shows Not Found correctly. But React Router itself logs
  one "could not be decoded" warning for it, and my code can't prevent that.
  The test allows exactly that one message and fails on anything else. Normal
  valid and invalid ids produce no warnings at all.

  Your move of Exercise 1.1 into EXERCISE/1.1 is still uncommitted; I left it
  alone.

❯ we start new peoject in folder 1.3 of folder EXERCISE,                        
  Exercise 1.3 — Legacy Validation Anti-Patterns                                
  Sprint 2 — Context-Driven Multi-Field Form Engine & Live ShowData Inspector   
  1. Sprint Overview                                                            
  Objective                                                                     
  Refactor the existing form validation architecture to eliminate unnecessary   
  useEffect chains and replace them with a synchronous, Context-driven          
  validation system.                                                            
                                                                                
  The application currently suffers from several architectural problems:        
                                                                                
  Multiple chained useEffect hooks for form state synchronization.              
                                                                                
  Redundant re-renders between InputField and SimpleForm.                       
                                                                                
  Race conditions caused by asynchronous effect execution.                      
                                                                                
  Infinite update loops caused by mutually dependent state updates.             
                                                                                
  Sprint 2 will establish a centralized form-state architecture using React     
  Context.                                                                      
                                                                                
  SimpleForm.jsx will act as the central form controller, while InputField.jsx  
  will consume the shared context to manage field values, validation results,   
  and error presentation.                                                       
                                                                                
  A successful form submission must also render a live <ShowData /> JSON        
  inspector containing the submitted form data.                                 
                                                                                
  2. Exact Sprint 2 Constraints                                                 
  The implementation MUST satisfy the following requirements:                   
                                                                                
  Fulfill the SimpleForm.jsx and InputField.jsx context contracts.              
                                                                                
  Implement synchronous validation without using useEffect for validation or    
  form-state synchronization.                                                   
                                                                                
  Eliminate cascading state synchronization between parent and child            
  components.                                                                   
                                                                                
  Prevent asynchronous validation race conditions.                              
                                                                                
  Render a live <ShowData /> JSON inspector after valid submission.             
                                                                                
  Complete exactly THREE atomic Git commits.                                    
                                                                                
  3. Core Architecture                                                          
  The application must follow this architectural responsibility:                
                                                                                
  Component    Responsibility                                                   
  SimpleForm.jsx    Central form controller and Context Provider                
  InputField.jsx    Consume form context and handle individual field            
  interactions                                                                  
  Validation functions    Perform synchronous field and form validation         
  ShowData.jsx    Display submitted form data as formatted JSON                 
  Do not introduce unnecessary state-management libraries or duplicate form     
  state across components.                                                      
  Final Sprint 2 Definition of Done                                             
  The exercise is considered complete only when all the following requirements  
  are satisfied.                                                                
                                                                                
  Architecture                                                                  
                                                                                
  SimpleForm.jsx implements the FormContext Provider.                           
                                                                                
                                                                                
  InputField.jsx consumes the shared context.                                   
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Centralized form state is implemented.                                        
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Centralized validation errors are implemented.                                
                                                                                
                                                                                
  No duplicated parent-child form state exists.                                 
                                                                                
  Validation                                                                    
                                                                                
  Field validation is synchronous.                                              
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Form validation is synchronous.                                               
                                                                                
                                                                                
  No useEffect is used for validation.                                          
                                                                                
                                                                                
  No useEffect is used for form-state synchronization.                          
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Required validation works.                                                    
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Custom validation rules work.                                                 
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Validation errors update correctly.                                           
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Invalid submissions are blocked.                                              
                                                                                
  Performance & Resiliency                                                      
                                                                                
  Legacy chained useEffect validation architecture is removed.                  
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Cascading effect-driven re-renders are eliminated.                            
                                                                                
                                                                                
  No asynchronous validation race conditions exist.                             
                                                                                
                                                                                
  No infinite update loops occur.                                               
                                                                                
                                                                                
  No Maximum update depth exceeded exceptions occur.                            
                                                                                
                                                                                
  No new console warnings or errors are introduced.                             
                                                                                
  ShowData Inspector                                                            
                                                                                
  ShowData.jsx is implemented.                                                  
                                                                                
                                                                                
  JSON is formatted using JSON.stringify.                                       
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Inspector appears after valid submission.                                     
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Invalid submissions do not create new successful output.                      
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Latest successful submission is displayed.                                    
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Inspector layout is readable and responsive.                                  
                                                                                
  Git Requirements                                                              
  Exactly THREE commits must be completed:                                      
                                                                                
  feat: form context                                                            
                                                                                
  feat: input field validation                                                  
                                                                                
  feat: inspector output                                                        
                                                                                
  No additional feature commits should be created for this Sprint 2 exercise.   
                                                                                
  7. Final Sprint Deliverable                                                   
  A fully functional Context-Driven Multi-Field Form Engine that replaces       
  legacy useEffect-based validation with synchronous centralized state          
  management.                                                                   
                                                                                
  The completed application must demonstrate:                                   
                                                                                
  Shared form state through React Context.                                      
                                                                                
  Controlled InputField components.                                             
                                                                                
  Synchronous field and form validation.                                        
                                                                                
  Elimination of redundant effect-driven state synchronization.                 
                                                                                
  Reliable valid/invalid submission handling.                                   
                                                                                
  A live ShowData JSON inspector displaying successfully submitted data.        
                                                                                
  Definition of Done: SimpleForm.jsx and InputField.jsx must fulfill their      
  Context contracts without useEffect-based validation, and successful          
  submissions must render the ShowData JSON inspector. All work must be         
  delivered through exactly three specified atomic Git commits.                 
  , and this is first PRDs                                                      
  PRD 01 — Centralized Form Context Architecture                                
  Development Task: Refactor SimpleForm into a centralized form-state           
  controller using React Context.                                               
                                                                                
  Git Commit: feat: form context                                                
                                                                                
  1. Objective                                                                  
  Establish a shared form context that allows all form fields to access and     
  update the same centralized form state.                                       
                                                                                
  SimpleForm.jsx must become the single source of truth for form values and     
  validation errors.                                                            
                                                                                
  The objective is to eliminate the legacy parent-child synchronization         
  architecture that relies on multiple chained useEffect hooks.                 
                                                                                
  2. Functional Requirements                                                    
  2.1 Create Form Context                                                       
  Create a dedicated React Context for form management.                         
                                                                                
  Use createContext() to establish the shared context.                          
                                                                                
  Export the context or an appropriate custom hook for consuming components.    
                                                                                
  Avoid introducing a separate state store.                                     
                                                                                
  Expected implementation pattern:                                              
                                                                                
  import {                                                                      
    createContext,                                                              
    useContext,                                                                 
    useState                                                                    
  } from 'react';                                                               
                                                                                
  const FormContext = createContext(null);                                      
                                                                                
  export function useFormContext() {                                            
    const context = useContext(FormContext);                                    
                                                                                
    if (!context) {                                                             
      throw new Error(                                                          
        'useFormContext must be used within SimpleForm'                         
      );                                                                        
    }                                                                           
                                                                                
    return context;                                                             
  }                                                                             
  2.2 Refactor SimpleForm.jsx                                                   
  SimpleForm.jsx must become the owner of the centralized form state.           
                                                                                
  Requirements:                                                                 
                                                                                
  Maintain a centralized values object.                                         
                                                                                
  Maintain a centralized errors object.                                         
                                                                                
  Provide a shared mechanism for updating field values.                         
                                                                                
  Provide a shared mechanism for updating validation errors.                    
                                                                                
  Provide the form submission handler.                                          
                                                                                
  Expose the necessary state and functions through FormContext.                 
                                                                                
  Expected state structure:                                                     
                                                                                
  const [values, setValues] = useState({});                                     
                                                                                
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});                                     
  The context must provide the necessary operations for child components to     
  interact with the centralized form state.                                     
                                                                                
  Example:                                                                      
                                                                                
  const formContextValue = {                                                    
    values,                                                                     
    errors,                                                                     
    handleChange,                                                               
    validateField,                                                              
    handleSubmit                                                                
  };                                                                            
  Adapt the context contract to the existing component interface.               
                                                                                
  2.3 Context Provider Integration                                              
  Wrap the form content using the context provider.                             
                                                                                
  Expected implementation:                                                      
                                                                                
  <FormContext.Provider value={formContextValue}>                               
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>                                              
      {children}                                                                
    </form>                                                                     
  </FormContext.Provider>                                                       
  Requirements:                                                                 
                                                                                
  All InputField components must access the same form context.                  
                                                                                
  Child components must not maintain duplicate copies of the parent form state. 
                                                                                
  Form state must remain centralized.                                           
                                                                                
  Preserve the existing form composition and children API where possible.       
                                                                                
  2.4 Eliminate Legacy Effect-Based Synchronization                             
  Remove unnecessary useEffect hooks responsible for:                           
                                                                                
  Copying InputField values into SimpleForm.                                    
                                                                                
  Synchronizing parent and child form states.                                   
                                                                                
  Updating validation errors after state changes.                               
                                                                                
  Triggering additional state updates based on other state updates.             
                                                                                
  Do not replace the old effect chain with another chain of effects.            
                                                                                
  3. Technical Constraints                                                      
  React Context API must be used.                                               
                                                                                
  useState is permitted for centralized form state.                             
                                                                                
  useContext must be used by consumers.                                         
                                                                                
  No useEffect for form-state synchronization.                                  
                                                                                
  Preserve existing application functionality.                                  
                                                                                
  Avoid unnecessary external dependencies.                                      
                                                                                
  4. Acceptance Criteria                                                        
                                                                                
  FormContext is created.                                                       
                                                                                
                                                                                
  A custom useFormContext() hook is implemented.                                
                                                                                
                                                                                
  SimpleForm.jsx acts as the central form controller.                           
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Centralized values state is implemented.                                      
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Centralized errors state is implemented.                                      
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Context Provider wraps the form children.                                     
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Context exposes the required form operations.                                 
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Legacy synchronization effects are removed.                                   
                                                                                
                                                                                
  No infinite state-update loops are introduced.                                
                                                                                
                                                                                
  Application compiles successfully.                                            
                                                                                
  5. Out of Scope                                                               
  Implementing the final ShowData inspector.                                    
                                                                                
  Building a new form design system.                                            
                                                                                
  Introducing Redux, Zustand, or another state-management library.              
                                                                                
  Implementing unrelated form features.

● PRD 01 is done and committed as 86e7775 feat: form context, the first of the
  sprint's three allowed commits. All 18 tests pass and the production build
  succeeds. The project is in LAB4/EXERCISE/1.3, in plain JSX like 1.2.

  src/components/SimpleForm.jsx:
  - const FormContext = createContext(null), plus useFormContext(), which throws
    "useFormContext must be used within SimpleForm" when used outside the form.
  - It owns both pieces of state: const [values, setValues] = useState(...) and
    const [errors, setErrors] = useState({}).
  - The context shares values, errors, handleChange, validateField,
    setFieldError, validateForm and handleSubmit. The provider wraps <form 
    onSubmit={handleSubmit}>{children}</form>, so you still compose the form by
    nesting fields inside it.
  - There's no useEffect anywhere. Every change and every validation happens
    inside the event handler that caused it, using the new values. If a field is
    showing an error, typing re-checks it straight away. That includes rules
    comparing two fields, like confirm password.
  - handleSubmit checks every rule. If anything fails it stops and records the
    errors; otherwise it passes a copy of the values to onSubmit.
  - Two updates at once: if two fields change in the same instant, both changes
    are kept; neither overwrites the other. A ref inside SimpleForm, updated by
    the handlers themselves, makes that safe.

  InputField.jsx is minimal for now. It keeps no copy of the value: it shows
  values[name] and writes through handleChange. Error display and validation
  behavior are PRD 02. The demo app is a registration form with full name,
  email, age, password and confirm password.

  Tests:
  - A React <Profiler> confirms one keystroke causes exactly one render, so
    there's no chain of extra re-renders.
  - Two changes in the same instant are both kept.
  - Invalid submits are blocked, valid ones submit a copy.
  - The whole app runs under StrictMode with no console warnings or errors.
  - Guard tests fail if useEffect/useLayoutEffect appears anywhere in src, if
    InputField gets its own state, or if a library like Redux or Zustand is
    added.

  Left for PRD 02: once a field's error is fixed, SimpleForm stops re-checking
  it. So if you later edit the other password field, the mismatch only shows
  again on submit. Fixing that needs "touched" tracking, which fits PRD 02's
  field validation.

  Since the sprint allows exactly three commits, I won't make any commit besides
  the two remaining ones. Ready for PRD 02.
